# Skills · H · part 10

768 entries, harmony-600 to harness-deployment. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **harmony-600**: Everything needed before working on the Harmony 600: where its facts are (read them first), how a configuration is written to the bench unit step by step, how a delay the remote saved is read and cleared, and how a bench test is run. - **harmony-analysis**: Harmonic analysis, voice leading, chord function, and modulation techniques for tonal music. Covers triads, seventh chords, Roman numeral analysis, figured bass, voice leading rules, cadence types, secondary dominants, modulation techniques, chromatic harmony (Neapolitan, augmented sixth chords), jazz lead sheet notation, and functional vs. linear harmonic thinking. Use when analyzing chord progressions, writing voice leading, identifying key areas, or interpreting harmonic function in any tonal style. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/music/harmony-analysis) · ★71)
